	"use strict";

	jQuery(document).ready(function() {
		var scrollFix = jQuery("#abthb");

		$(window).on("scroll", function(e) {

			if ($(window).scrollTop() > 300) {
				scrollFix.addClass("fixedVar");
				scrollFix.removeClass("out-of-screen");
			} else if ($(window).scrollTop() < 300 && $(window).scrollTop() > 50) {
				scrollFix.addClass("out-of-screen");
			} else {
				scrollFix.removeClass("fixedVar");
				scrollFix.removeClass("out-of-screen");
			}

		})
	});

<div class="textAlignC">
<span class="boxSubTitlePreviewSEO grey">We are cyclists, runners, swimmers, triathletes, hikers, and gym-goers. We understand how it feels to GET THERE - that feeling when, whatever your sport, whatever your level, you go further, push harder, get better, and the impossible suddenly feels like home.</span>
<span class="boxSubTitlePreviewSEO grey">Like you, we’ve overcome our barriers, including the snooze button. Mostly. We feel the same rush of hitting that peak, climbing that gradient, and beating that time. 
We’ve had days we’d like to forget but still can’t wait to get out there again. 
We savour those training days with our mates, and the solitary sessions that clear our heads.</span>
<span class="boxSubTitlePreviewSEO grey">	We’re on your local park run; we’ve swam lengths beside you at the pool; we’ve met you on a 100-mile ride and hiked past you on a mountainside.
This is why we’re experts in sourcing, testing, and delivering the best sports equipment there is at extraordinary prices. And why the world’s biggest names choose to work with us while we continue to support small, specialist brands doing amazing work in sports tech and innovation. That’s why Wiggle works. Because, like you, we’re just trying to be the best we can be.</span>
<div class="textAlignC" style="margin-bottom:50px;">
<span class="boxSubTitleSEO grey">So let’s Get There together.</span>

                    var agent_domain = egainDockChat.serverURL;
                    var agent_availability_uri = "/egain/chat/entrypoint/agentAvailability";
                    var agent_capacity_uri = "/egain/chat/entrypoint/capacity";
                    var eligibility_uri = "/egain/chat/entrypoint/checkEligibility";
                    var entryPointId = egainDockChat.EntryPointId;
                    var eglvchathandle = null;
                    var availability_url = agent_domain + '/' + agent_availability_uri + '/' + entryPointId;
                    var capacity_url = agent_domain + '/' + agent_capacity_uri + '/' + entryPointId;
                    var eligibility_url = agent_domain + '/' + eligibility_uri + '/' + entryPointId;

                    $.ajax({
                        type: 'GET',
                        url: eligibility_url,
                        dataType: 'xml',
                        success: function (xml) {
                            var responseType = $(xml).find('checkEligibility').attr('responseType');

                            if (responseType === "0") {
                                setLiveChatAvailable();
                            } else if (responseType === "1") {
                                setLiveChatUnavailable();
                            } else if (responseType === "2") {
                                setLiveChatBusy();
                            }
                        }
                    });

                } catch (err) {
                    console.log('Live Chat API error');
                    ga('send', 'exception', {
                        'exDescription': err.message,
                        'exFatal': false
                    });
                }
            }

            $(document).ready(function () {
                checkEGainChatAvailablity();
            });
        })();

    </script>
